define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t603 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/asyncwrite.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t605 = alloca i32
  %t604 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t603, ptr %t605, ptr null)
  %t606 = icmp ne i32 %t604, 0
  br i1 %t606, label %main.async.fail.80, label %main.async.ok.80

  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.80:
  %t607 = load i32, ptr %t605
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t607
}
